Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in geometry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 253 and the invariant I(A,B) = 622 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 253 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the geometry structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 622. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
